Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The algebras variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 427 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 95 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the algebras decomposition. By minimality, the element a^427 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 95-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
